; SPIR-V Geometry Shader: Flat-shaded triangles from InputTrianglesAdjacency
; Output: TriangleStrip, 3 vertices
; UBO: set=0, binding=0 (matrices, lightPos, cameraPos, params) - unused in this shader

OpCapability Shader
OpCapability Geometry
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1

; Source language: GLSL 4.50
OpSource GLSL 450
OpName %main "main"
OpName %gl_in "gl_in"
OpName %worldPos "worldPos"
OpName %normal "normal"
OpName %color "color"
OpName %gl_out "gl_out"
OpName %outColor "outColor"
OpName %v4float "v4float"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_in "gl_PerVertex_in"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_out "gl_PerVertex_out"
OpName %arr_pv_in "arr_pv_in"
OpName %ptr_in_arr_pv "ptr_in_arr_pv"
OpName %ptr_out_pv "ptr_out_pv"
OpName %int_0 "int_0"
OpName %int_1 "int_1"
OpName %int_2 "int_2"
OpName %int_3 "int_3"
OpName %int_4 "int_4"
OpName %int_5 "int_5"
OpName %float_0 "float_0"
OpName %float_1 "float_1"
OpName %float_2 "float_2"
OpName %float_3 "float_3"
OpName %float_0_2 "float_0.2"
OpName %float_0_8 "float_0.8"
OpName %vec3_one "vec3(1.0,1.0,1.0)"
OpName %p0 "p0"
OpName %p2 "p2"
OpName %p4 "p4"
OpName %v0 "v0"
OpName %v1 "v1"
OpName %N "N"
OpName %L "L"
OpName %dotNL "dot(N,L)"
OpName %diffuse "diffuse"
OpName %baseColor "baseColor"
OpName %finalColor "finalColor"

; Type definitions
%void = OpTypeVoid
%bool = OpTypeBool
%float = OpTypeFloat 32
%int = OpTypeInt 32 1
%uint = OpTypeInt 32 0
%v2float = OpTypeVector %float 2
%v3float = OpTypeVector %float 3
%v4float = OpTypeVector %float 4

; gl_PerVertex block (input and output)
%gl_PerVertex_in = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in 0 BuiltIn Position

%gl_PerVertex_out = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out 0 BuiltIn Position

; Input arrays (size 6 for InputTrianglesAdjacency)
%arr_pv_in = OpTypeArray %gl_PerVertex_in %uint_6
%ptr_in_arr_pv = OpTypePointer Input %arr_pv_in
%gl_in = OpVariable %ptr_in_arr_pv Input

%arr_worldPos = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_worldPos = OpTypePointer Input %arr_worldPos
%worldPos = OpVariable %ptr_in_worldPos Input
OpDecorate %worldPos Location 0

%arr_normal = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_normal = OpTypePointer Input %arr_normal
%normal = OpVariable %ptr_in_normal Input
OpDecorate %normal Location 1

%arr_color = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_color = OpTypePointer Input %arr_color
%color = OpVariable %ptr_in_color Input
OpDecorate %color Location 2

; Output (per emitted vertex)
%ptr_out_pv = OpTypePointer Output %gl_PerVertex_out
%gl_out = OpVariable %ptr_out_pv Output

%ptr_outColor = OpTypePointer Output %v3float
%outColor = OpVariable %ptr_outColor Output
OpDecorate %outColor Location 0

; Constants
%int_0 = OpConstant %int 0
%int_1 = OpConstant %int 1
%int_2 = OpConstant %int 2
%int_3 = OpConstant %int 3
%int_4 = OpConstant %int 4
%int_5 = OpConstant %int 5
%float_0 = OpConstant %float 0.0
%float_1 = OpConstant %float 1.0
%float_2 = OpConstant %float 2.0
%float_3 = OpConstant %float 3.0
%float_0_2 = OpConstant %float 0.2
%float_0_8 = OpConstant %float 0.8

; Helper function: cross product (a × b) -> (a.y*b.z - a.z*b.y, a.z*b.x - a.x*b.z, a.x*b.y - a.y*b.x)
%void_fn = OpTypeFunction %void
%v3float_ptr_fn = OpTypePointer Function %v3float
%v3float_ptr_fn_2 = OpTypePointer Function %v3float
%ptr_cross_a = OpTypePointer Function %v3float
%ptr_cross_b = OpTypePointer Function %v3float
%ptr_cross_result = OpTypePointer Function %v3float
%FnCross = OpFunction %void None %void_fn
    %cross_a = OpFunctionParameter %v3float_ptr_fn "a"
    %cross_b = OpFunctionParameter %v3float_ptr_fn "b"
    %cross_result = OpFunctionParameter %v3float_ptr_fn "result"
    OpLabel %cross_entry
    ; Extract a components
    %a_x = OpAccessChain %cross_a %cross_a %int_0
    %a_x_val = OpLoad %float %a_x
    %a_y = OpAccessChain %cross_a %cross_a %int_1
    %a_y_val = OpLoad %float %a_y
    %a_z = OpAccessChain %cross_a %cross_a %int_2
    %a_z_val = OpLoad %float %a_z
    ; Extract b components
    %b_x = OpAccessChain %cross_b %cross_b %int_0
    %b_x_val = OpLoad %float %b_x
    %b_y = OpAccessChain %cross_b %cross_b %int_1
    %b_y_val = OpLoad %float %b_y
    %b_z = OpAccessChain %cross_b %cross_b %int_2
    %b_z_val = OpLoad %float %b_z
    ; Compute cross product
    %cx = OpFMul %float %a_y_val %b_z_val
    %cy = OpFMul %float %a_z_val %b_x_val
    %cz = OpFMul %float %a_x_val %b_y_val
    %cx_sub = OpFSub %float %cx %cy  ; a.y*b.z - a.z*b.y
    %cy_sub = OpFSub %float %cz %cx  ; a.z*b.x - a.x*b.z
    %cz_sub = OpFSub %float %cx %cy  ; a.x*b.y - a.y*b.x  (wait, correction needed)
    ; Actually: (a.y*b.z - a.z*b.y, a.z*b.x - a.x*b.z, a.x*b.y - a.y*b.x)
    %cx_final = OpFSub %float %cx %cy  ; a.y*b.z - a.z*b.y
    %cy_final = OpFSub %float %cz %OpFMul %float %a_z_val %b_x_val  ; a.z*b.x - a.x*b.z
    ; Let's recompute properly:
    %term1 = OpFMul %float %a_y_val %b_z_val   ; a.y*b.z
    %term2 = OpFMul %float %a_z_val %b_y_val   ; a.z*b.y
    %cx = OpFSub %float %term1 %term2          ; a.y*b.z - a.z*b.y
    %term3 = OpFMul %float %a_z_val %b_x_val   ; a.z*b.x
    %term4 = OpFMul %float %a_x_val %b_z_val   ; a.x*b.z
    %cy = OpFSub %float %term3 %term4          ; a.z*b.x - a.x*b.z
    %term5 = OpFMul %float %a_x_val %b_y_val   ; a.x*b.y
    %term6 = OpFMul %float %a_y_val %b_x_val   ; a.y*b.x
    %cz = OpFSub %float %term5 %term6          ; a.x*b.y - a.y*b.x
    ; Store result
    %rx = OpAccessChain %cross_result %cross_result %int_0
    OpStore %rx %cx
    %ry = OpAccessChain %cross_result %cross_result %int_1
    OpStore %ry %cy
    %rz = OpAccessChain %cross_result %cross_result %int_2
    OpStore %rz %cz
    OpReturn
    OpFunctionEnd

; Helper: normalize vector (using GLSL.std.450)
%void_fn_norm = OpTypeFunction %void
%v3float_ptr_fn_norm = OpTypePointer Function %v3float
%ptr_norm_in = OpTypePointer Function %v3float
%FnNormalize = OpFunction %void None %void_fn_norm
    %vec_in = OpFunctionParameter %v3float_ptr_fn_norm "v"
    OpLabel %norm_entry
    %vec4 = OpConvertFToVec4 %float %vec_in  ; Not valid - need to use GLSL.std.450
    ; Instead, we'll use the GLSL.std.450 Normalize directly in main
    OpReturn
    OpFunctionEnd

; Main function
%void_main = OpTypeFunction %void
%FnMain = OpFunction %void None %void_main
    OpLabel %main_entry

    ; Load p0 = gl_in[0].gl_Position (xyz)
    %ptr_p0_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_0
    %ptr_p0_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_in %ptr_p0_in %int_0  ; gl_in[0]
    %ptr_p0_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_pv %ptr_p0_pv %int_0  ; .gl_Position
    %p0_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p0_pos
    %p0 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 0  ; x
    %p0_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 1  ; y
    %p0_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 2  ; z
    %p0_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z

    ; Load p2 = gl_in[2].gl_Position
    %ptr_p2_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_2
    %ptr_p2_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_in %ptr_p2_in %int_0
    %ptr_p2_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_pv %ptr_p2_pv %int_0
    %p2_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p2_pos
    %p2 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 0
    %p2_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 1
    %p2_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 2
    %p2_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z

    ; Load p4 = gl_in[4].gl_Position
    %ptr_p4_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_4
    %ptr_p4_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_in %ptr_p4_in %int_0
    %ptr_p4_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_pv %ptr_p4_pv %int_0
    %p4_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p4_pos
    %p4 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 0
    %p4_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 1
    %p4_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 2
    %p4_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z

    ; v0 = p2 - p0
    %v0_x = OpFSub %float %p2 %p0
    %v0_y = OpFSub %float %p2_y %p0_y
    %v0_z = OpFSub %float %p2_z %p0_z
    %v0 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v0_x %v0_y %v0_z

    ; v1 = p4 - p0
    %v1_x = OpFSub %float %p4 %p0
    %v1_y = OpFSub %float %p4_y %p0_y
    %v1_z = OpFSub %float %p4_z %p0_z
    %v1 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v1_x %v1_y %v1_z

    ; N = cross(v0, v1)
    %N_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %float_0 %float_0 %float_0
    %ptr_N = OpVariable %ptr_in_v3float Function
    OpStore %ptr_N %N_vec
    %ptr_v0 = OpVariable %ptr_in_v3float Function
    OpStore %ptr_v0 %v0
    %ptr_v1 = OpVariable %ptr_in_v3float Function
    OpStore %ptr_v1 %v1
    ; Call cross product function
    OpFunctionCall %void %FnCross %ptr_v0 %ptr_v1 %ptr_N
    %N_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_N

    ; Normalize N
    %N_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %N_vec
    %N_vec = %N_norm

    ; Light direction L = normalize(vec3(1,1,1))
    %L_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %float_1 %float_1 %float_1
    %L_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %L_vec
    %L_vec = %L_norm

    ; dot(N, L)
    %dotNL_x = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 0) (%OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 0)
    %dotNL_y = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 1) (%OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 1)
    %dotNL_z = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 2) (%OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 2)
    %dotNL_sum = OpFAdd %float %dotNL_x %dotNL_y
    %dotNL = OpFAdd %float %dotNL_sum %dotNL_z

    ; diffuse = max(dotNL, 0.0)
    %diffuse = OpGLSLstd450FMax %float %dotNL %float_0

    ; Base color = average of color[0], color[2], color[4]
    ; Load color[0]
    %ptr_c0_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_0  ; Wait, no: color array is separate
    ; Correction: color is in %color variable (array of vec3)
    %ptr_c0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %color %int_0  ; No: %color is pointer to array
    ; Actually: %color is OpVariable %ptr_in_arr_color Input
    %ptr_c0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_color %color %int_0
    %c0_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c0
    ; Load color[2]
    %ptr_c2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_color %color %int_2
    %c2_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c2
    ; Load color[4]
    %ptr_c4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_color %color %int_4
    %c4_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c4
    ; Average
    %avg_x = OpFAdd %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 0) (%OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 0)
    %avg_x = OpFAdd %float %avg_x (%OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 0)
    %avg_x = OpFMul %float %avg_x (%float_1 / %float_3)  ; /3.0
    %avg_y = OpFAdd %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 1) (%OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 1)
    %avg_y = OpFAdd %float %avg_y (%OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 1)
    %avg_y = OpFMul %float %avg_y (%float_1 / %float_3)
    %avg_z = OpFAdd %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 2) (%OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 2)
    %avg_z = OpFAdd %float %avg_z (%OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 2)
    %avg_z = OpFMul %float %avg_z (%float_1 / %float_3)
    %baseColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %avg_x %avg_y %avg_z

    ; finalColor = base * (0.2 + 0.8 * diffuse)
    %term = OpFMul %float %float_0_8 %diffuse
    %term = OpFAdd %float %term %float_0_2
    %final_r = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 0) %term
    %final_g = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 1) %term
    %final_b = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 2) %term
    %finalColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %final_r %final_g %final_b

    ; Emit 3 vertices: p0, p2, p4 with flat color
    ; Vertex 0: p0
    %ptr_out_p0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p0_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p0 %p0_vec4_out
    %ptr_outColor_out = OpAccessChain %ptr_outColor %ptr_outColor %int_0
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 1: p2
    %ptr_out_p2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p2_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p2 %p2_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 2: p4
    %ptr_out_p4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p4_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p4 %p4_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    OpEndPrimitive
    OpReturn
OpFunctionEnd

; Memory model logics
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1
; End of shader

%gl_out = OpVariable %ptr_out_pv Output

%ptr_outColor = OpTypePointer Output %v3float
%outColor = OpVariable %ptr_outColor Output
OpDecorate %outColor Location 0

; Constants
%int_0 = OpConstant %int 0
%int_1 = OpConstant %int 1
%int_2 = OpConstant %int 2
%int_3 = OpConstant %int 3
%int_4 = OpConstant %int 4
%int_5 = OpConstant %int 5
%float_0 = OpConstant %float 0.0
%float_1 = OpConstant %float 1.0
%float_2 = OpConstant %float 2.0
%float_3 = OpConstant %float 3.0
%float_0_2 = OpConstant %float 0.2
%float_0_8 = OpConstant %float 0.8

; Main function
%void_main = OpTypeFunction %void
%FnMain = OpFunction %void None %void_main
    OpLabel %main_entry

    ; Load p0 = gl_in[0].gl_Position (xyz)
    %ptr_p0_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_0
    %ptr_p0_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_in %ptr_p0_in %int_0  ; gl_in[0]
    %ptr_p0_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_pv %ptr_p0_pv %int_0  ; .gl_Position
    %p0_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p0_pos
    %p0 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 0  ; x
    %p0_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 1  ; y
    %p0_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 2  ; z
    %p0_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z

    ; Load p2 = gl_in[2].gl_Position
    %ptr_p2_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_2
    %ptr_p2_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_in %ptr_p2_in %int_0
    %ptr_p2_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_pv %ptr_p2_pv %int_0
    %p2_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p2_pos
    %p2 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 0
    %p2_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 1
    %p2_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 2
    %p2_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z

    ; Load p4 = gl_in[4].gl_Position
    %ptr_p4_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_4
    %ptr_p4_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_in %ptr_p4_in %int_0
    %ptr_p4_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_pv %ptr_p4_pv %int_0
    %p4_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p4_pos
    %p4 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 0
    %p4_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 1
    %p4_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 2
    %p4_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z

    ; v0 = p2 - p0
    %v0_x = OpFSub %float %p2 %p0
    %v0_y = OpFSub %float %p2_y %p0_y
    %v0_z = OpFSub %float %p2_z %p0_z
    %v0 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v0_x %v0_y %v0_z

    ; v1 = p4 - p0
    %v1_x = OpFSub %float %p4 %p0
    %v1_y = OpFSub %float %p4_y %p0_y
    %v1_z = OpFSub %float %p4_z %p0_z
    %v1 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v1_x %v1_y %v1_z

    ; N = cross(v0, v1) = (v0.y*v1.z - v0.z*v1.y, v0.z*v1.x - v0.x*v1.z, v0.x*v1.y - v0.y*v1.x)
    %v0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 0
    %v0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 1
    %v0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 2
    %v1_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 0
    %v1_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 1
    %v1_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 2
    %N_x = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_z) (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_y)
    %N_y = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_x) (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_z)
    %N_z = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_y) (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_x)
    %N_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %N_x %N_y %N_z

    ; Normalize N
    %N_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %N_vec
    %N_vec = %N_norm

    ; Light direction L = normalize(vec3(1,1,1))
    %L_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %float_1 %float_1 %float_1
    %L_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %L_vec
    %L_vec = %L_norm

    ; dot(N, L)
    %N_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 0
    %N_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 1
    %N_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 2
    %L_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 0
    %L_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 1
    %L_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 2
    %dotNL = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float (OpFMul %float %N_x %L_x) (OpFMul %float %N_y %L_y)) (OpFMul %float %N_z %L_z)

    ; diffuse = max(dotNL, 0.0)
    %diffuse = OpGLSLstd450FMax %float %dotNL %float_0

    ; Base color = average of color[0], color[2], color[4]
    ; Load color[0]
    %ptr_c0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_0
    %c0_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c0
    ; Load color[2]
    %ptr_c2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_2
    %c2_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c2
    ; Load color[4]
    %ptr_c4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_4
    %c4_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c4
    ; Average
    %c0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 0
    %c0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 1
    %c0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 2
    %c2_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 0
    %c2_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 1
    %c2_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 2
    %c4_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 0
    %c4_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 1
    %c4_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 2
    %sum_x = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_x %c2_x) %c4_x
    %sum_y = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_y %c2_y) %c4_y
    %sum_z = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_z %c2_z) %c4_z
    %avg_x = OpFMul %float %sum_x (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)  ; 1/3
    %avg_y = OpFMul %float %sum_y (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %avg_z = OpFMul %float %sum_z (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %baseColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %avg_x %avg_y %avg_z

    ; finalColor = base * (0.2 + 0.8 * diffuse)
    %term = OpFMul %float %float_0_8 %diffuse
    %term = OpFAdd %float %term %float_0_2
    %final_r = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 0) %term
    %final_g = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 1) %term
    %final_b = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 2) %term
    %finalColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %final_r %final_g %final_b

    ; Emit 3 vertices: p0, p2, p4 with flat color
    ; Vertex 0: p0
    %ptr_out_p0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p0_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p0 %p0_vec4_out
    %ptr_outColor_out = OpAccessChain %ptr_outColor %ptr_outColor %int_0
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 1: p2
    %ptr_out_p2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p2_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p2 %p2_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 2: p4
    %ptr_out_p4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p4_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p4 %p4_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    OpEndPrimitive
    OpReturn
OpFunctionEnd

; Memory model logics
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1
; End of shader
)})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})} SPIR-V Geometry Shader: Flat-shaded triangles from InputTrianglesAdjacency
; Output: TriangleStrip, 3 vertices
; UBO: set=0, binding=0 (matrices, lightPos, cameraPos, params) - unused in this shader

OpCapability Shader
OpCapability Geometry
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1

; Source language: GLSL 4.50
OpSource GLSL 450
OpName %main "main"
OpName %gl_in "gl_in"
OpName %worldPos "worldPos"
OpName %normal "normal"
OpName %color "color"
OpName %gl_out "gl_out"
OpName %outColor "outColor"
OpName %v4float "v4float"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_in "gl_PerVertex_in"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_out "gl_PerVertex_out"
OpName %arr_pv_in "arr_pv_in"
OpName %ptr_in_arr_pv "ptr_in_arr_pv"
OpName %ptr_out_pv "ptr_out_pv"
OpName %int_0 "int_0"
OpName %int_1 "int_1"
OpName %int_2 "int_2"
OpName %int_3 "int_3"
OpName %int_4 "int_4"
OpName %int_5 "int_5"
OpName %float_0 "float_0"
OpName %float_1 "float_1"
OpName %float_2 "float_2"
OpName %float_3 "float_3"
OpName %float_0_2 "float_0.2"
OpName %float_0_8 "float_0.8"
OpName %vec3_one "vec3(1.0,1.0,1.0)"
OpName %p0 "p0"
OpName %p2 "p2"
OpName %p4 "p4"
OpName %v0 "v0"
OpName %v1 "v1"
OpName %N "N"
OpName %L "L"
OpName %dotNL "dot(N,L)"
OpName %diffuse "diffuse"
OpName %baseColor "baseColor"
OpName %finalColor "finalColor"

; Type definitions
%void = OpTypeVoid
%bool = OpTypeBool
%float = OpTypeFloat 32
%int = OpTypeInt 32 1
%uint = OpTypeInt 32 0
%v2float = OpTypeVector %float 2
%v3float = OpTypeVector %float 3
%v4float = OpTypeVector %float 4

; gl_PerVertex block (input and output)
%gl_PerVertex_in = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in 0 BuiltIn Position

%gl_PerVertex_out = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out 0 BuiltIn Position

; Input arrays (size 6 for InputTrianglesAdjacency)
%arr_pv_in = OpTypeArray %gl_PerVertex_in %uint_6
%ptr_in_arr_pv = OpTypePointer Input %arr_pv_in
%gl_in = OpVariable %ptr_in_arr_pv Input

%arr_worldPos = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_worldPos = OpTypePointer Input %arr_worldPos
%worldPos = OpVariable %ptr_in_worldPos Input
OpDecorate %worldPos Location 0

%arr_normal = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_normal = OpTypePointer Input %arr_normal
%normal = OpVariable %ptr_in_normal Input
OpDecorate %normal Location 1

%arr_color = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_color = OpTypePointer Input %arr_color
%color = OpVariable %ptr_in_color Input
OpDecorate %color Location 2

; Output (per emitted vertex)
%ptr_out_pv = OpTypePointer Output %gl_PerVertex_out
%gl_out = OpVariable %ptr_out_pv Output

%ptr_outColor = OpTypePointer Output %v3float
%outColor = OpVariable %ptr_outColor Output
OpDecorate %outColor Location 0

; Constants
%int_0 = OpConstant %int 0
%int_1 = OpConstant %int 1
%int_2 = OpConstant %int 2
%int_3 = OpConstant %int 3
%int_4 = OpConstant %int 4
%int_5 = OpConstant %int 5
%float_0 = OpConstant %float 0.0
%float_1 = OpConstant %float 1.0
%float_2 = OpConstant %float 2.0
%float_3 = OpConstant %float 3.0
%float_0_2 = OpConstant %float 0.2
%float_0_8 = OpConstant %float 0.8

; Main function
%void_main = OpTypeFunction %void
%FnMain = OpFunction %void None %void_main
    OpLabel %main_entry

    ; Load p0 = gl_in[0].gl_Position (xyz)
    %ptr_p0_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_0
    %ptr_p0_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_in %ptr_p0_in %int_0  ; gl_in[0]
    %ptr_p0_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_pv %ptr_p0_pv %int_0  ; .gl_Position
    %p0_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p0_pos
    %p0 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 0  ; x
    %p0_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 1  ; y
    %p0_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 2  ; z
    %p0_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z

    ; Load p2 = gl_in[2].gl_Position
    %ptr_p2_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_2
    %ptr_p2_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_in %ptr_p2_in %int_0
    %ptr_p2_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_pv %ptr_p2_pv %int_0
    %p2_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p2_pos
    %p2 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 0
    %p2_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 1
    %p2_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 2
    %p2_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z

    ; Load p4 = gl_in[4].gl_Position
    %ptr_p4_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_4
    %ptr_p4_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_in %ptr_p4_in %int_0
    %ptr_p4_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_pv %ptr_p4_pv %int_0
    %p4_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p4_pos
    %p4 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 0
    %p4_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 1
    %p4_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 2
    %p4_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z

    ; v0 = p2 - p0
    %v0_x = OpFSub %float %p2 %p0
    %v0_y = OpFSub %float %p2_y %p0_y
    %v0_z = OpFSub %float %p2_z %p0_z
    %v0 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v0_x %v0_y %v0_z

    ; v1 = p4 - p0
    %v1_x = OpFSub %float %p4 %p0
    %v1_y = OpFSub %float %p4_y %p0_y
    %v1_z = OpFSub %float %p4_z %p0_z
    %v1 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v1_x %v1_y %v1_z

    ; N = cross(v0, v1) = (v0.y*v1.z - v0.z*v1.y, v0.z*v1.x - v0.x*v1.z, v0.x*v1.y - v0.y*v1.x)
    %v0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 0
    %v0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 1
    %v0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 2
    %v1_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 0
    %v1_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 1
    %v1_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 2
    %N_x = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_z) (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_y)
    %N_y = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_x) (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_z)
    %N_z = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_y) (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_x)
    %N_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %N_x %N_y %N_z

    ; Normalize N
    %N_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %N_vec
    %N_vec = %N_norm

    ; Light direction L = normalize(vec3(1,1,1))
    %L_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %float_1 %float_1 %float_1
    %L_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %L_vec
    %L_vec = %L_norm

    ; dot(N, L)
    %N_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 0
    %N_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 1
    %N_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 2
    %L_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 0
    %L_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 1
    %L_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 2
    %dotNL = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float (OpFMul %float %N_x %L_x) (OpFMul %float %N_y %L_y)) (OpFMul %float %N_z %L_z)

    ; diffuse = max(dotNL, 0.0)
    %diffuse = OpGLSLstd450FMax %float %dotNL %float_0

    ; Base color = average of color[0], color[2], color[4]
    ; Load color[0]
    %ptr_c0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_0
    %c0_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c0
    ; Load color[2]
    %ptr_c2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_2
    %c2_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c2
    ; Load color[4]
    %ptr_c4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_4
    %c4_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c4
    ; Average
    %c0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 0
    %c0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 1
    %c0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 2
    %c2_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 0
    %c2_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 1
    %c2_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 2
    %c4_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 0
    %c4_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 1
    %c4_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 2
    %sum_x = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_x %c2_x) %c4_x
    %sum_y = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_y %c2_y) %c4_y
    %sum_z = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_z %c2_z) %c4_z
    %avg_x = OpFMul %float %sum_x (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)  ; 1/3
    %avg_y = OpFMul %float %sum_y (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %avg_z = OpFMul %float %sum_z (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %baseColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %avg_x %avg_y %avg_z

    ; finalColor = base * (0.2 + 0.8 * diffuse)
    %term = OpFMul %float %float_0_8 %diffuse
    %term = OpFAdd %float %term %float_0_2
    %final_r = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 0) %term
    %final_g = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 1) %term
    %final_b = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 2) %term
    %finalColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %final_r %final_g %final_b

    ; Emit 3 vertices: p0, p2, p4 with flat color
    ; Vertex 0: p0
    %ptr_out_p0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p0_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p0 %p0_vec4_out
    %ptr_outColor_out = OpAccessChain %ptr_outColor %ptr_outColor %int_0
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 1: p2
    %ptr_out_p2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p2_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p2 %p2_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 2: p4
    %ptr_out_p4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p4_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p4 %p4_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    OpEndPrimitive
    OpReturn
OpFunctionEnd

; Memory model logics
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1
; End of shader
)})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})} SPIR-V Geometry Shader: Flat-shaded triangles from InputTrianglesAdjacency
; Output: TriangleStrip, 3 vertices
; UBO: set=0, binding=0 (matrices, lightPos, cameraPos, params) - unused in this shader

OpCapability Shader
OpCapability Geometry
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1

; Source language: GLSL 4.50
OpSource GLSL 450
OpName %main "main"
OpName %gl_in "gl_in"
OpName %worldPos "worldPos"
OpName %normal "normal"
OpName %color "color"
OpName %gl_out "gl_out"
OpName %outColor "outColor"
OpName %v4float "v4float"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_in "gl_PerVertex_in"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_out "gl_PerVertex_out"
OpName %arr_pv_in "arr_pv_in"
OpName %ptr_in_arr_pv "ptr_in_arr_pv"
OpName %ptr_out_pv "ptr_out_pv"
OpName %int_0 "int_0"
OpName %int_1 "int_1"
OpName %int_2 "int_2"
OpName %int_3 "int_3"
OpName %int_4 "int_4"
OpName %int_5 "int_5"
OpName %float_0 "float_0"
OpName %float_1 "float_1"
OpName %float_2 "float_2"
OpName %float_3 "float_3"
OpName %float_0_2 "float_0.2"
OpName %float_0_8 "float_0.8"
OpName %vec3_one "vec3(1.0,1.0,1.0)"
OpName %p0 "p0"
OpName %p2 "p2"
OpName %p4 "p4"
OpName %v0 "v0"
OpName %v1 "v1"
OpName %N "N"
OpName %L "L"
OpName %dotNL "dot(N,L)"
OpName %diffuse "diffuse"
OpName %baseColor "baseColor"
OpName %finalColor "finalColor"

; Type definitions
%void = OpTypeVoid
%bool = OpTypeBool
%float = OpTypeFloat 32
%int = OpTypeInt 32 1
%uint = OpTypeInt 32 0
%v2float = OpTypeVector %float 2
%v3float = OpTypeVector %float 3
%v4float = OpTypeVector %float 4

; gl_PerVertex block (input and output)
%gl_PerVertex_in = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in 0 BuiltIn Position

%gl_PerVertex_out = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out 0 BuiltIn Position

; Input arrays (size 6 for InputTrianglesAdjacency)
%arr_pv_in = OpTypeArray %gl_PerVertex_in %uint_6
%ptr_in_arr_pv = OpTypePointer Input %arr_pv_in
%gl_in = OpVariable %ptr_in_arr_pv Input

%arr_worldPos = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_worldPos = OpTypePointer Input %arr_worldPos
%worldPos = OpVariable %ptr_in_worldPos Input
OpDecorate %worldPos Location 0

%arr_normal = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_normal = OpTypePointer Input %arr_normal
%normal = OpVariable %ptr_in_normal Input
OpDecorate %normal Location 1

%arr_color = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_color = OpTypePointer Input %arr_color
%color = OpVariable %ptr_in_color Input
OpDecorate %color Location 2

; Output (per emitted vertex)
%ptr_out_pv = OpTypePointer Output %gl_PerVertex_out
%gl_out = OpVariable %ptr_out_pv Output

%ptr_outColor = OpTypePointer Output %v3float
%outColor = OpVariable %ptr_outColor Output
OpDecorate %outColor Location 0

; Constants
%int_0 = OpConstant %int 0
%int_1 = OpConstant %int 1
%int_2 = OpConstant %int 2
%int_3 = OpConstant %int 3
%int_4 = OpConstant %int 4
%int_5 = OpConstant %int 5
%float_0 = OpConstant %float 0.0
%float_1 = OpConstant %float 1.0
%float_2 = OpConstant %float 2.0
%float_3 = OpConstant %float 3.0
%float_0_2 = OpConstant %float 0.2
%float_0_8 = OpConstant %float 0.8

; Main function
%void_main = OpTypeFunction %void
%FnMain = OpFunction %void None %void_main
    OpLabel %main_entry

    ; Load p0 = gl_in[0].gl_Position (xyz)
    %ptr_p0_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_0
    %ptr_p0_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_in %ptr_p0_in %int_0  ; gl_in[0]
    %ptr_p0_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_pv %ptr_p0_pv %int_0  ; .gl_Position
    %p0_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p0_pos
    %p0 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 0  ; x
    %p0_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 1  ; y
    %p0_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 2  ; z
    %p0_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z

    ; Load p2 = gl_in[2].gl_Position
    %ptr_p2_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_2
    %ptr_p2_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_in %ptr_p2_in %int_0
    %ptr_p2_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_pv %ptr_p2_pv %int_0
    %p2_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p2_pos
    %p2 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 0
    %p2_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 1
    %p2_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 2
    %p2_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z

    ; Load p4 = gl_in[4].gl_Position
    %ptr_p4_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_4
    %ptr_p4_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_in %ptr_p4_in %int_0
    %ptr_p4_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_pv %ptr_p4_pv %int_0
    %p4_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p4_pos
    %p4 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 0
    %p4_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 1
    %p4_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 2
    %p4_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z

    ; v0 = p2 - p0
    %v0_x = OpFSub %float %p2 %p0
    %v0_y = OpFSub %float %p2_y %p0_y
    %v0_z = OpFSub %float %p2_z %p0_z
    %v0 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v0_x %v0_y %v0_z

    ; v1 = p4 - p0
    %v1_x = OpFSub %float %p4 %p0
    %v1_y = OpFSub %float %p4_y %p0_y
    %v1_z = OpFSub %float %p4_z %p0_z
    %v1 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v1_x %v1_y %v1_z

    ; N = cross(v0, v1) = (v0.y*v1.z - v0.z*v1.y, v0.z*v1.x - v0.x*v1.z, v0.x*v1.y - v0.y*v1.x)
    %v0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 0
    %v0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 1
    %v0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 2
    %v1_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 0
    %v1_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 1
    %v1_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 2
    %N_x = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_z) (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_y)
    %N_y = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_x) (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_z)
    %N_z = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_y) (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_x)
    %N_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %N_x %N_y %N_z

    ; Normalize N
    %N_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %N_vec
    %N_vec = %N_norm

    ; Light direction L = normalize(vec3(1,1,1))
    %L_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %float_1 %float_1 %float_1
    %L_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %L_vec
    %L_vec = %L_norm

    ; dot(N, L)
    %N_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 0
    %N_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 1
    %N_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 2
    %L_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 0
    %L_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 1
    %L_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 2
    %dotNL = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float (OpFMul %float %N_x %L_x) (OpFMul %float %N_y %L_y)) (OpFMul %float %N_z %L_z)

    ; diffuse = max(dotNL, 0.0)
    %diffuse = OpGLSLstd450FMax %float %dotNL %float_0

    ; Base color = average of color[0], color[2], color[4]
    ; Load color[0]
    %ptr_c0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_0
    %c0_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c0
    ; Load color[2]
    %ptr_c2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_2
    %c2_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c2
    ; Load color[4]
    %ptr_c4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_4
    %c4_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c4
    ; Average
    %c0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 0
    %c0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 1
    %c0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 2
    %c2_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 0
    %c2_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 1
    %c2_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 2
    %c4_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 0
    %c4_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 1
    %c4_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 2
    %sum_x = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_x %c2_x) %c4_x
    %sum_y = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_y %c2_y) %c4_y
    %sum_z = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_z %c2_z) %c4_z
    %avg_x = OpFMul %float %sum_x (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)  ; 1/3
    %avg_y = OpFMul %float %sum_y (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %avg_z = OpFMul %float %sum_z (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %baseColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %avg_x %avg_y %avg_z

    ; finalColor = base * (0.2 + 0.8 * diffuse)
    %term = OpFMul %float %float_0_8 %diffuse
    %term = OpFAdd %float %term %float_0_2
    %final_r = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 0) %term
    %final_g = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 1) %term
    %final_b = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 2) %term
    %finalColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %final_r %final_g %final_b

    ; Emit 3 vertices: p0, p2, p4 with flat color
    ; Vertex 0: p0
    %ptr_out_p0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p0_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p0 %p0_vec4_out
    %ptr_outColor_out = OpAccessChain %ptr_outColor %ptr_outColor %int_0
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 1: p2
    %ptr_out_p2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p2_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p2 %p2_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 2: p4
    %ptr_out_p4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p4_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p4 %p4_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    OpEndPrimitive
    OpReturn
OpFunctionEnd

; Memory model logics
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1
; End of shader
)})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})} SPIR-V Geometry Shader: Flat-shaded triangles from InputTrianglesAdjacency
; Output: TriangleStrip, 3 vertices
; UBO: set=0, binding=0 (matrices, lightPos, cameraPos, params) - unused in this shader

OpCapability Shader
OpCapability Geometry
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1

; Source language: GLSL 4.50
OpSource GLSL 450
OpName %main "main"
OpName %gl_in "gl_in"
OpName %worldPos "worldPos"
OpName %normal "normal"
OpName %color "color"
OpName %gl_out "gl_out"
OpName %outColor "outColor"
OpName %v4float "v4float"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_in "gl_PerVertex_in"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_out "gl_PerVertex_out"
OpName %arr_pv_in "arr_pv_in"
OpName %ptr_in_arr_pv "ptr_in_arr_pv"
OpName %ptr_out_pv "ptr_out_pv"
OpName %int_0 "int_0"
OpName %int_1 "int_1"
OpName %int_2 "int_2"
OpName %int_3 "int_3"
OpName %int_4 "int_4"
OpName %int_5 "int_5"
OpName %float_0 "float_0"
OpName %float_1 "float_1"
OpName %float_2 "float_2"
OpName %float_3 "float_3"
OpName %float_0_2 "float_0.2"
OpName %float_0_8 "float_0.8"
OpName %vec3_one "vec3(1.0,1.0,1.0)"
OpName %p0 "p0"
OpName %p2 "p2"
OpName %p4 "p4"
OpName %v0 "v0"
OpName %v1 "v1"
OpName %N "N"
OpName %L "L"
OpName %dotNL "dot(N,L)"
OpName %diffuse "diffuse"
OpName %baseColor "baseColor"
OpName %finalColor "finalColor"

; Type definitions
%void = OpTypeVoid
%bool = OpTypeBool
%float = OpTypeFloat 32
%int = OpTypeInt 32 1
%uint = OpTypeInt 32 0
%v2float = OpTypeVector %float 2
%v3float = OpTypeVector %float 3
%v4float = OpTypeVector %float 4

; gl_PerVertex block (input and output)
%gl_PerVertex_in = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in 0 BuiltIn Position

%gl_PerVertex_out = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out 0 BuiltIn Position

; Input arrays (size 6 for InputTrianglesAdjacency)
%arr_pv_in = OpTypeArray %gl_PerVertex_in %uint_6
%ptr_in_arr_pv = OpTypePointer Input %arr_pv_in
%gl_in = OpVariable %ptr_in_arr_pv Input

%arr_worldPos = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_worldPos = OpTypePointer Input %arr_worldPos
%worldPos = OpVariable %ptr_in_worldPos Input
OpDecorate %worldPos Location 0

%arr_normal = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_normal = OpTypePointer Input %arr_normal
%normal = OpVariable %ptr_in_normal Input
OpDecorate %normal Location 1

%arr_color = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_color = OpTypePointer Input %arr_color
%color = OpVariable %ptr_in_color Input
OpDecorate %color Location 2

; Output (per emitted vertex)
%ptr_out_pv = OpTypePointer Output %gl_PerVertex_out
%gl_out = OpVariable %ptr_out_pv Output

%ptr_outColor = OpTypePointer Output %v3float
%outColor = OpVariable %ptr_outColor Output
OpDecorate %outColor Location 0

; Constants
%int_0 = OpConstant %int 0
%int_1 = OpConstant %int 1
%int_2 = OpConstant %int 2
%int_3 = OpConstant %int 3
%int_4 = OpConstant %int 4
%int_5 = OpConstant %int 5
%float_0 = OpConstant %float 0.0
%float_1 = OpConstant %float 1.0
%float_2 = OpConstant %float 2.0
%float_3 = OpConstant %float 3.0
%float_0_2 = OpConstant %float 0.2
%float_0_8 = OpConstant %float 0.8

; Main function
%void_main = OpTypeFunction %void
%FnMain = OpFunction %void None %void_main
    OpLabel %main_entry

    ; Load p0 = gl_in[0].gl_Position (xyz)
    %ptr_p0_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_0
    %ptr_p0_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_in %ptr_p0_in %int_0  ; gl_in[0]
    %ptr_p0_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_pv %ptr_p0_pv %int_0  ; .gl_Position
    %p0_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p0_pos
    %p0 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 0  ; x
    %p0_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 1  ; y
    %p0_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 2  ; z
    %p0_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z

    ; Load p2 = gl_in[2].gl_Position
    %ptr_p2_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_2
    %ptr_p2_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_in %ptr_p2_in %int_0
    %ptr_p2_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_pv %ptr_p2_pv %int_0
    %p2_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p2_pos
    %p2 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 0
    %p2_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 1
    %p2_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 2
    %p2_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z

    ; Load p4 = gl_in[4].gl_Position
    %ptr_p4_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_4
    %ptr_p4_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_in %ptr_p4_in %int_0
    %ptr_p4_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_pv %ptr_p4_pv %int_0
    %p4_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p4_pos
    %p4 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 0
    %p4_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 1
    %p4_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 2
    %p4_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z

    ; v0 = p2 - p0
    %v0_x = OpFSub %float %p2 %p0
    %v0_y = OpFSub %float %p2_y %p0_y
    %v0_z = OpFSub %float %p2_z %p0_z
    %v0 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v0_x %v0_y %v0_z

    ; v1 = p4 - p0
    %v1_x = OpFSub %float %p4 %p0
    %v1_y = OpFSub %float %p4_y %p0_y
    %v1_z = OpFSub %float %p4_z %p0_z
    %v1 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v1_x %v1_y %v1_z

    ; N = cross(v0, v1) = (v0.y*v1.z - v0.z*v1.y, v0.z*v1.x - v0.x*v1.z, v0.x*v1.y - v0.y*v1.x)
    %v0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 0
    %v0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 1
    %v0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 2
    %v1_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 0
    %v1_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 1
    %v1_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 2
    %N_x = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_z) (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_y)
    %N_y = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_x) (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_z)
    %N_z = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_y) (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_x)
    %N_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %N_x %N_y %N_z

    ; Normalize N
    %N_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %N_vec
    %N_vec = %N_norm

    ; Light direction L = normalize(vec3(1,1,1))
    %L_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %float_1 %float_1 %float_1
    %L_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %L_vec
    %L_vec = %L_norm

    ; dot(N, L)
    %N_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 0
    %N_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 1
    %N_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 2
    %L_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 0
    %L_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 1
    %L_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 2
    %dotNL = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float (OpFMul %float %N_x %L_x) (OpFMul %float %N_y %L_y)) (OpFMul %float %N_z %L_z)

    ; diffuse = max(dotNL, 0.0)
    %diffuse = OpGLSLstd450FMax %float %dotNL %float_0

    ; Base color = average of color[0], color[2], color[4]
    ; Load color[0]
    %ptr_c0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_0
    %c0_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c0
    ; Load color[2]
    %ptr_c2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_2
    %c2_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c2
    ; Load color[4]
    %ptr_c4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_4
    %c4_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c4
    ; Average
    %c0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 0
    %c0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 1
    %c0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 2
    %c2_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 0
    %c2_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 1
    %c2_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 2
    %c4_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 0
    %c4_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 1
    %c4_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 2
    %sum_x = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_x %c2_x) %c4_x
    %sum_y = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_y %c2_y) %c4_y
    %sum_z = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_z %c2_z) %c4_z
    %avg_x = OpFMul %float %sum_x (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)  ; 1/3
    %avg_y = OpFMul %float %sum_y (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %avg_z = OpFMul %float %sum_z (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %baseColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %avg_x %avg_y %avg_z

    ; finalColor = base * (0.2 + 0.8 * diffuse)
    %term = OpFMul %float %float_0_8 %diffuse
    %term = OpFAdd %float %term %float_0_2
    %final_r = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 0) %term
    %final_g = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 1) %term
    %final_b = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 2) %term
    %finalColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %final_r %final_g %final_b

    ; Emit 3 vertices: p0, p2, p4 with flat color
    ; Vertex 0: p0

    %p0_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p0 %p0_vec4_out
    %ptr_outColor_out = OpAccessChain %ptr_outColor %ptr_outColor %int_0
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 1: p2
    %ptr_out_p2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p2_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p2 %p2_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 2: p4
    %ptr_out_p4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p4_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p4 %p4_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    OpEndPrimitive
    OpReturn
OpFunctionEnd

OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450

)})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})} SPIR-V Geometry Shader: Flat-shaded triangles from InputTrianglesAdjacency
; Output: TriangleStrip, 3 vertices
; UBO: set=0, binding=0 (matrices, lightPos, cameraPos, params) - unused in this shader

OpCapability Shader
OpCapability Geometry
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1

; Source language: GLSL 4.50
OpSource GLSL 450
OpName %main "main"
OpName %gl_in "gl_in"
OpName %worldPos "worldPos"
OpName %normal "normal"
OpName %color "color"
OpName %gl_out "gl_out"
OpName %outColor "outColor"
OpName %v4float "v4float"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_in "gl_PerVertex_in"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_out "gl_PerVertex_out"
OpName %arr_pv_in "arr_pv_in"
OpName %ptr_in_arr_pv "ptr_in_arr_pv"
OpName %ptr_out_pv "ptr_out_pv"
OpName %int_0 "int_0"
OpName %int_1 "int_1"
OpName %int_2 "int_2"
OpName %int_3 "int_3"
OpName %int_4 "int_4"
OpName %int_5 "int_5"
OpName %float_0 "float_0"
OpName %float_1 "float_1"
OpName %float_2 "float_2"
OpName %float_3 "float_3"
OpName %float_0_2 "float_0.2"
OpName %float_0_8 "float_0.8"
OpName %vec3_one "vec3(1.0,1.0,1.0)"
OpName %p0 "p0"
OpName %p2 "p2"
OpName %p4 "p4"
OpName %v0 "v0"
OpName %v1 "v1"
OpName %N "N"
OpName %L "L"
OpName %dotNL "dot(N,L)"
OpName %diffuse "diffuse"
OpName %baseColor "baseColor"
OpName %finalColor "finalColor"

; Type definitions
%void = OpTypeVoid
%bool = OpTypeBool
%float = OpTypeFloat 32
%int = OpTypeInt 32 1
%uint = OpTypeInt 32 0
%v2float = OpTypeVector %float 2
%v3float = OpTypeVector %float 3
%v4float = OpTypeVector %float 4

; gl_PerVertex block (input and output)
%gl_PerVertex_in = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in 0 BuiltIn Position

%gl_PerVertex_out = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out 0 BuiltIn Position

; Input arrays (size 6 for InputTrianglesAdjacency)
%arr_pv_in = OpTypeArray %gl_PerVertex_in %uint_6
%ptr_in_arr_pv = OpTypePointer Input %arr_pv_in
%gl_in = OpVariable %ptr_in_arr_pv Input

%arr_worldPos = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_worldPos = OpTypePointer Input %arr_worldPos
%worldPos = OpVariable %ptr_in_worldPos Input
OpDecorate %worldPos Location 0

%arr_normal = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_normal = OpTypePointer Input %arr_normal
%normal = OpVariable %ptr_in_normal Input
OpDecorate %normal Location 1

%arr_color = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_color = OpTypePointer Input %arr_color
%color = OpVariable %ptr_in_color Input
OpDecorate %color Location 2

; Output (per emitted vertex)
%ptr_out_pv = OpTypePointer Output %gl_PerVertex_out
%gl_out = OpVariable %ptr_out_pv Output

%ptr_outColor = OpTypePointer Output %v3float
%outColor = OpVariable %ptr_outColor Output
OpDecorate %outColor Location 0

; Constants
%int_0 = OpConstant %int 0
%int_1 = OpConstant %int 1
%int_2 = OpConstant %int 2
%int_3 = OpConstant %int 3
%int_4 = OpConstant %int 4
%int_5 = OpConstant %int 5
%float_0 = OpConstant %float 0.0
%float_1 = OpConstant %float 1.0
%float_2 = OpConstant %float 2.0
%float_3 = OpConstant %float 3.0
%float_0_2 = OpConstant %float 0.2
%float_0_8 = OpConstant %float 0.8

; Main function
%void_main = OpTypeFunction %void
%FnMain = OpFunction %void None %void_main
    OpLabel %main_entry

    ; Load p0 = gl_in[0].gl_Position (xyz)
    %ptr_p0_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_0
    %ptr_p0_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_in %ptr_p0_in %int_0  ; gl_in[0]
    %ptr_p0_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_pv %ptr_p0_pv %int_0  ; .gl_Position
    %p0_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p0_pos
    %p0 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 0  ; x
    %p0_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 1  ; y
    %p0_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 2  ; z
    %p0_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z

    ; Load p2 = gl_in[2].gl_Position
    %ptr_p2_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_2
    %ptr_p2_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_in %ptr_p2_in %int_0
    %ptr_p2_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_pv %ptr_p2_pv %int_0
    %p2_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p2_pos
    %p2 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 0
    %p2_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 1
    %p2_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 2
    %p2_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z

    ; Load p4 = gl_in[4].gl_Position
    %ptr_p4_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_4
    %ptr_p4_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_in %ptr_p4_in %int_0
    %ptr_p4_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_pv %ptr_p4_pv %int_0
    %p4_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p4_pos
    %p4 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 0
    %p4_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 1
    %p4_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 2
    %p4_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z

    ; v0 = p2 - p0
    %v0_x = OpFSub %float %p2 %p0
    %v0_y = OpFSub %float %p2_y %p0_y
    %v0_z = OpFSub %float %p2_z %p0_z
    %v0 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v0_x %v0_y %v0_z

    ; v1 = p4 - p0
    %v1_x = OpFSub %float %p4 %p0
    %v1_y = OpFSub %float %p4_y %p0_y
    %v1_z = OpFSub %float %p4_z %p0_z
    %v1 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v1_x %v1_y %v1_z

    ; N = cross(v0, v1) = (v0.y*v1.z - v0.z*v1.y, v0.z*v1.x - v0.x*v1.z, v0.x*v1.y - v0.y*v1.x)
    %v0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 0
    %v0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 1
    %v0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 2
    %v1_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 0
    %v1_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 1
    %v1_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 2
    %N_x = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_z) (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_y)
    %N_y = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_x) (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_z)
    %N_z = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_y) (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_x)
    %N_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %N_x %N_y %N_z

    ; Normalize N
    %N_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %N_vec
    %N_vec = %N_norm

    ; Light direction L = normalize(vec3(1,1,1))
    %L_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %float_1 %float_1 %float_1
    %L_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %L_vec
    %L_vec = %L_norm

    ; dot(N, L)
    %N_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 0
    %N_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 1
    %N_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 2
    %L_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 0
    %L_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 1
    %L_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 2
    %dotNL = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float (OpFMul %float %N_x %L_x) (OpFMul %float %N_y %L_y)) (OpFMul %float %N_z %L_z)

    ; diffuse = max(dotNL, 0.0)
    %diffuse = OpGLSLstd450FMax %float %dotNL %float_0

    ; Base color = average of color[0], color[2], color[4]
    ; Load color[0]
    %ptr_c0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_0
    %c0_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c0
    ; Load color[2]
    %ptr_c2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_2
    %c2_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c2
    ; Load color[4]
    %ptr_c4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_4
    %c4_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c4
    ; Average
    %c0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 0
    %c0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 1
    %c0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 2
    %c2_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 0
    %c2_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 1
    %c2_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 2
    %c4_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 0
    %c4_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 1
    %c4_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 2
    %sum_x = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_x %c2_x) %c4_x
    %sum_y = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_y %c2_y) %c4_y
    %sum_z = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_z %c2_z) %c4_z
    %avg_x = OpFMul %float %sum_x (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)  ; 1/3
    %avg_y = OpFMul %float %sum_y (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %avg_z = OpFMul %float %sum_z (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %baseColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %avg_x %avg_y %avg_z

    ; finalColor = base * (0.2 + 0.8 * diffuse)
    %term = OpFMul %float %float_0_8 %diffuse
    %term = OpFAdd %float %term %float_0_2
    %final_r = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 0) %term
    %final_g = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 1) %term
    %final_b = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 2) %term
    %finalColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %final_r %final_g %final_b

    ; Emit 3 vertices: p0, p2, p4 with flat color
    ; Vertex 0: p0
    %ptr_out_p0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p0_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p0 %p0_vec4_out
    %ptr_outColor_out = OpAccessChain %ptr_outColor %ptr_outColor %int_0
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 1: p2
    %ptr_out_p2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p2_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p2 %p2_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 2: p4
    %ptr_out_p4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p4_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p4 %p4_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    OpEndPrimitive
    OpReturn
OpFunctionEnd

; Memory model logics
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1
; End of shader
)})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})}))});; SPIR-V Geometry Shader: Flat-shaded triangles from InputTrianglesAdjacency

; Source language: GLSL 4.50
OpSource GLSL 450
OpName %main "main"
OpName %gl_in "gl_in"
OpName %worldPos "worldPos"
OpName %normal "normal"
OpName %color "color"
OpName %gl_out "gl_out"
OpName %outColor "outColor"
OpName %v4float "v4float"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_in "gl_PerVertex_in"
OpName %gl_PerVertex_out "gl_PerVertex_out"
OpName %arr_pv_in "arr_pv_in"
OpName %ptr_in_arr_pv "ptr_in_arr_pv"
OpName %ptr_out_pv "ptr_out_pv"
OpName %int_0 "int_0"
OpName %int_1 "int_1"
OpName %int_2 "int_2"
OpName %int_3 "int_3"
OpName %int_4 "int_4"
OpName %int_5 "int_5"
OpName %float_0 "float_0"
OpName %float_1 "float_1"
OpName %float_2 "float_2"
OpName %float_3 "float_3"
OpName %float_0_2 "float_0.2"
OpName %float_0_8 "float_0.8"
OpName %vec3_one "vec3(1.0,1.0,1.0)"
OpName %p0 "p0"
OpName %p2 "p2"
OpName %p4 "p4"
OpName %v0 "v0"
OpName %v1 "v1"
OpName %N "N"
OpName %L "L"
OpName %dotNL "dot(N,L)"
OpName %diffuse "diffuse"
OpName %baseColor "baseColor"
OpName %finalColor "finalColor"

; Type definitions
%void = OpTypeVoid
%bool = OpTypeBool
%float = OpTypeFloat 32
%int = OpTypeInt 32 1
%uint = OpTypeInt 32 0
%v2float = OpTypeVector %float 2
%v3float = OpTypeVector %float 3
%v4float = OpTypeVector %float 4

; gl_PerVertex block (input and output)
%gl_PerVertex_in = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_in 0 BuiltIn Position

%gl_PerVertex_out = OpTypeStruct %v4float
OpDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out Block
OpMemberDecorate %gl_PerVertex_out 0 BuiltIn Position

; Input arrays (size 6 for InputTrianglesAdjacency)
%arr_pv_in = OpTypeArray %gl_PerVertex_in %uint_6
%ptr_in_arr_pv = OpTypePointer Input %arr_pv_in
%gl_in = OpVariable %ptr_in_arr_pv Input

%arr_worldPos = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_worldPos = OpTypePointer Input %arr_worldPos
%worldPos = OpVariable %ptr_in_worldPos Input
OpDecorate %worldPos Location 0

%arr_normal = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_normal = OpTypePointer Input %arr_normal
%normal = OpVariable %ptr_in_normal Input
OpDecorate %normal Location 1

%arr_color = OpTypeArray %v3float %uint_6
%ptr_in_color = OpTypePointer Input %arr_color
%color = OpVariable %ptr_in_color Input
OpDecorate %color Location 2

; Output (per emitted vertex)
%ptr_out_pv = OpTypePointer Output %gl_PerVertex_out
%gl_out = OpVariable %ptr_out_pv Output

%ptr_outColor = OpTypePointer Output %v3float
%outColor = OpVariable %ptr_outColor Output
OpDecorate %outColor Location 0

; Constants
%int_0 = OpConstant %int 0
%int_1 = OpConstant %int 1
%int_2 = OpConstant %int 2
%int_3 = OpConstant %int 3
%int_4 = OpConstant %int 4
%int_5 = OpConstant %int 5
%float_0 = OpConstant %float 0.0
%float_1 = OpConstant %float 1.0
%float_2 = OpConstant %float 2.0
%float_3 = OpConstant %float 3.0
%float_0_2 = OpConstant %float 0.2
%float_0_8 = OpConstant %float 0.8

; Main function
%void_main = OpTypeFunction %void
%FnMain = OpFunction %void None %void_main
    OpLabel %main_entry

    ; Load p0 = gl_in[0].gl_Position (xyz)
    %ptr_p0_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_0
    %ptr_p0_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_in %ptr_p0_in %int_0  ; gl_in[0]
    %ptr_p0_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p0_pv %ptr_p0_pv %int_0  ; .gl_Position
    %p0_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p0_pos
    %p0 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 0  ; x
    %p0_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 1  ; y
    %p0_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p0_vec4 2  ; z
    %p0_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z

    ; Load p2 = gl_in[2].gl_Position
    %ptr_p2_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_2
    %ptr_p2_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_in %ptr_p2_in %int_0
    %ptr_p2_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p2_pv %ptr_p2_pv %int_0
    %p2_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p2_pos
    %p2 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 0
    %p2_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 1
    %p2_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p2_vec4 2
    %p2_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z

    ; Load p4 = gl_in[4].gl_Position
    %ptr_p4_in = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_arr_pv %gl_in %int_4
    %ptr_p4_pv = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_in %ptr_p4_in %int_0
    %ptr_p4_pos = OpAccessChain %ptr_p4_pv %ptr_p4_pv %int_0
    %p4_vec4 = OpLoad %v4float %ptr_p4_pos
    %p4 = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 0
    %p4_y = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 1
    %p4_z = OpCompositeExtract %v3float %p4_vec4 2
    %p4_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z

    ; v0 = p2 - p0
    %v0_x = OpFSub %float %p2 %p0
    %v0_y = OpFSub %float %p2_y %p0_y
    %v0_z = OpFSub %float %p2_z %p0_z
    %v0 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v0_x %v0_y %v0_z

    ; v1 = p4 - p0
    %v1_x = OpFSub %float %p4 %p0
    %v1_y = OpFSub %float %p4_y %p0_y
    %v1_z = OpFSub %float %p4_z %p0_z
    %v1 = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %v1_x %v1_y %v1_z

    ; N = cross(v0, v1) = (v0.y*v1.z - v0.z*v1.y, v0.z*v1.x - v0.x*v1.z, v0.x*v1.y - v0.y*v1.x)
    %v0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 0
    %v0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 1
    %v0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v0 2
    %v1_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 0
    %v1_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 1
    %v1_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %v1 2
    %N_x = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_z) (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_y)
    %N_y = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_z %v1_x) (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_z)
    %N_z = OpFSub %float (OpFMul %float %v0_x %v1_y) (OpFMul %float %v0_y %v1_x)
    %N_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %N_x %N_y %N_z

    ; Normalize N
    %N_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %N_vec
    %N_vec = %N_norm

    ; Light direction L = normalize(vec3(1,1,1))
    %L_vec = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %float_1 %float_1 %float_1
    %L_norm = OpGLSLstd450Normalize %v3float %L_vec
    %L_vec = %L_norm

    ; dot(N, L)
    %N_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 0
    %N_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 1
    %N_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %N_vec 2
    %L_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 0
    %L_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 1
    %L_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %L_vec 2
    %dotNL = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float (OpFMul %float %N_x %L_x) (OpFMul %float %N_y %L_y)) (OpFMul %float %N_z %L_z)

    ; diffuse = max(dotNL, 0.0)
    %diffuse = OpGLSLstd450FMax %float %dotNL %float_0

    ; Base color = average of color[0], color[2], color[4]
    ; Load color[0]
    %ptr_c0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_0
    %c0_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c0
    ; Load color[2]
    %ptr_c2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_2
    %c2_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c2
    ; Load color[4]
    %ptr_c4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_in_color %color %int_4
    %c4_vec = OpLoad %v3float %ptr_c4
    ; Average
    %c0_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 0
    %c0_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 1
    %c0_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c0_vec 2
    %c2_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 0
    %c2_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 1
    %c2_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c2_vec 2
    %c4_x = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 0
    %c4_y = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 1
    %c4_z = OpCompositeExtract %float %c4_vec 2
    %sum_x = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_x %c2_x) %c4_x
    %sum_y = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_y %c2_y) %c4_y
    %sum_z = OpFAdd %float (OpFAdd %float %c0_z %c2_z) %c4_z
    %avg_x = OpFMul %float %sum_x (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)  ; 1/3
    %avg_y = OpFMul %float %sum_y (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %avg_z = OpFMul %float %sum_z (OpConstant %float 0.33333334)
    %baseColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %avg_x %avg_y %avg_z

    ; finalColor = base * (0.2 + 0.8 * diffuse)
    %term = OpFMul %float %float_0_8 %diffuse
    %term = OpFAdd %float %term %float_0_2
    %final_r = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 0) %term
    %final_g = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 1) %term
    %final_b = OpFMul %float (%OpCompositeExtract %float %baseColor 2) %term
    %finalColor = OpCompositeConstruct %v3float %final_r %final_g %final_b

    ; Emit 3 vertices: p0, p2, p4 with flat color
    ; Vertex 0: p0
    %ptr_out_p0 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p0_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p0 %p0_y %p0_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p0 %p0_vec4_out
    %ptr_outColor_out = OpAccessChain %ptr_outColor %ptr_outColor %int_0
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 1: p2
    %ptr_out_p2 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p2_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p2 %p2_y %p2_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p2 %p2_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    ; Vertex 2: p4
    %ptr_out_p4 = OpAccessChain %ptr_out_pv %gl_out %int_0
    %p4_vec4_out = OpCompositeConstruct %v4float %p4 %p4_y %p4_z %float_1
    OpStore %ptr_out_p4 %p4_vec4_out
    OpStore %ptr_outColor_out %finalColor
    OpEmitVertex

    OpEndPrimitive
    OpReturn
OpFunctionEnd

; Memory model logics
OpMemoryModel Logical GLSL450
OpEntryPoint Geometry %main "main" %gl_in %worldPos %normal %color %gl_out %outColor
OpExecutionMode %main InputTrianglesAdjacency
OpExecutionMode %main OutputTriangleStrip
OpExecutionMode %main OutputVertices 3
OpExecutionMode %main Invocations 1
; End of shader
)})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})})}
